'use client'

import { AddWordsModal } from "@/components/features/vocab/AddWordsModal";
import { GroupList } from "@/components/features/vocab/group/GroupList";
import { PracticeHistoryTabContent } from "@/components/features/vocab/PracticeHistoryTabContent";
import { WordBankListItem as WordBankListItemComponent } from "@/components/features/vocab/WordBankListItem";
import { ShareWordBankDialog } from "@/components/features/vocab/ShareWordBankDialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import useLocalStorage from "@/hooks/useLocalStorage";
import { api, type RouterOutputs } from "@/utils/trpc";
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { AlertCircle, BookText, Grid, History, List, Pencil, Play, Plus, Settings, SortAsc, SortDesc, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "next-auth/react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

// Extend word bank type to include groupId for admin check
type WordBankListItem = RouterOutputs['vocab']['listWordBanks'][number] & {
  lastPracticedAt?: Date | null;
  isShared?: boolean;
  groupId?: string | null; // Add groupId as optional
};

type SortKey = 'name' | 'createdAt' | 'masteryPercentage';
type SortDirection = 'asc' | 'desc';

type SessionStatus = 'loading' | 'authenticated' | 'unauthenticated';

function CreateWordBankButton({ isFullWidth = false }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session, status } = useSession();
  const sessionStatus = status as SessionStatus;

  const handleCreateClick = () => {
    if (sessionStatus === 'loading') {
      toast.info('Loading session information...');
      return;
    }
    if (!session) {
      toast.warning('You need to be logged in to create a word bank.');
      router.push('/login?callbackUrl=/vocabulary/create-bank');
      return;
    }
    router.push('/vocabulary/create-bank');
  };

  if (!session) {
    return null;
  }
  
  return (
    <Button
      variant="outline"
      className={isFullWidth ? "w-full mt-4 md:mt-0" : "mt-4"}
      onClick={handleCreateClick}
      disabled={sessionStatus === 'loading'}
    >
      {sessionStatus === 'loading' ? (
        'Loading...'
      ) : (
        isFullWidth ? (
          <><Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Create New Bank</>
        ) : (
          'Create Word Bank'
        )
      )}
    </Button>
  );
}

interface WordBankCardProps {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string | null;
  wordCount: number;
  lastPracticedAt?: Date | null;
  masteryPercentage: number;
  isShared?: boolean;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
}

function WordBankCard({
  id,
  title,
  description,
  wordCount,
  lastPracticedAt,
  masteryPercentage,
  isShared = false,
  isAdmin = false,
}: WordBankCardProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const startSessionMutation = api.vocab.startVocabSession.useMutation({
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      toast.success('Practice session started!');
      router.push(`/vocabulary/practice/${data.practiceSessionId}`);
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      toast.error(`Failed to start session: ${error.message}`);
    }
  });

  const handleStartPractice = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    startSessionMutation.mutate({ wordBankId: id });
  };

  return (
    <Card className="flex flex-col h-full">
     <Link href={`/vocabulary/banks/${id}`} className="flex flex-col flex-grow hover:bg-muted/50 rounded-t-lg">
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <BookText className="h-5 w-5" />
            {title}
            {isShared && <Users className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />}
          </span>
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription className="line-clamp-3 h-[3.75rem] mt-1">
          {description || `Last practiced: ${lastPracticedAt ? format(new Date(lastPracticedAt), 'PP') : 'Never'}`}
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="flex-grow pt-2">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-sm font-medium">
            <span>Mastery</span>
            <span>{masteryPercentage}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-2 bg-secondary rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div className="h-full bg-primary rounded-full" style={{ width: `${masteryPercentage}%` }} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
     </Link>
      <CardFooter className="flex justify-between items-center pt-4">
        {wordCount === 0 ? (
          <AddWordsModal bankId={id}>
            <Button variant="outline" className="w-full border-cyan-500 text-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-500/10 hover:text-cyan-600 flex-grow mr-2">
              <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Add Words
            </Button>
          </AddWordsModal>
        ) : (
          <Button
            variant="default"
            className="w-full flex-grow mr-2 bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-600 text-white"
            onClick={handleStartPractice}
            disabled={startSessionMutation.isPending}
          >
            {startSessionMutation.isPending
              ? 'Starting...'
              : <><Play className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Practice</>
            }
          </Button>
        )}
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/banks/${id}`} passHref>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" title="Manage Words">
             <Settings className="h-4 w-4" />
             <span className="sr-only">Manage Words</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/edit/${id}`} className="ml-1">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" title="Edit Bank">
            <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span className="sr-only">Edit Bank</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        {/* Conditionally render ShareWordBankDialog based on isAdmin */}
        {isAdmin && (
          <ShareWordBankDialog groupId={undefined} wordBanks={[{ id, name: title, description, wordCount }]} />
        )}
      </CardFooter>
    </Card>
  )
}

export default function VocabularyPage() {
  // Use useLocalStorage for viewMode persistence
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useLocalStorage<'grid' | 'list'>('vocabViewMode', 'grid');
  const [filterText, setFilterText] = useState('');
  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState<SortKey>('createdAt');
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<SortDirection>('desc');

  // Fetch word banks
  const { data: wordBanks, isLoading: isLoadingBanks, error: banksError } = api.vocab.listWordBanks.useQuery(undefined, {
    select: (data) => data.map(bank => ({
      ...bank,
      isShared: 'isShared' in bank ? Boolean(bank.isShared) : false
    }))
  })

  // Fetch user's groups for admin status
  const { data: userGroups } = api.group.getUsersGroups.useQuery();

  // Determine admin group IDs for the user
  const adminGroupIds = userGroups?.filter(g => g.members?.some(m => m.userId === g.creatorId && m.role === 'admin')).map(g => g.id) ?? [];

  // Client-side filtering and sorting
  const filteredAndSortedBanks = useMemo(() => {
    if (!wordBanks) return [];
    let banks = [...wordBanks];

    // Filter
    if (filterText) {
      banks = banks.filter(bank =>
        bank.name.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase()) ||
        bank.description?.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    // Sort
    banks.sort((a, b) => {
      let compareA: string | number | Date = a[sortKey];
      let compareB: string | number | Date = b[sortKey];

      if (sortKey === 'createdAt') {
        compareA = new Date(compareA);
        compareB = new Date(compareB);
      }

      let comparison = 0;
      if (compareA > compareB) comparison = 1;
      else if (compareA < compareB) comparison = -1;

      return sortDirection === 'desc' ? comparison * -1 : comparison;
    });

    return banks;
  }, [wordBanks, filterText, sortKey, sortDirection]);

  const toggleSortDirection = () => {
    setSortDirection(prev => prev === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc');
  }

  // Add this near the top of the component
  const { data: session, status: authStatus } = useSession();

  return (
    <main className="container mx-auto px-4 py-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Vocabulary Dashboard</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1">Manage your word banks and track your progress.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Overview Section - Placeholder */}
            {/*
            <Card className="mb-8">
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>Overall Progress</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground">Progress metrics coming soon...</p>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
            */}

      {/* Word Banks Section */}
      <div className="mt-8 mb-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Your Word Banks</h2>
          {/* Filter, Sort, View Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full md:w-auto">
             <Input
               placeholder="Filter banks..."
               value={filterText}
               onChange={(e) => setFilterText(e.target.value)}
               className="h-9 max-w-xs"
             />
             <Select value={sortKey} onValueChange={(value) => setSortKey(value as SortKey)}>
               <SelectTrigger className="h-9 w-[150px]">
                 <SelectValue placeholder="Sort by" />
               </SelectTrigger>
               <SelectContent>
                 <SelectItem value="name">Name</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="createdAt">Date Created</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="masteryPercentage">Mastery</SelectItem>
               </SelectContent>
             </Select>
             <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={toggleSortDirection} className="h-9 w-9">
               {sortDirection === 'asc' ? <SortAsc className="h-4 w-4" /> : <SortDesc className="h-4 w-4" />}
             </Button>
             <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-muted p-1 ml-2">
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'grid' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('grid')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="Grid view">
                 <Grid className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'list' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('list')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="List view">
                 <List className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
             </div>
           </div>
        </div>

        {/* Loading State */}
        {isLoadingBanks && (
          <div className={viewMode === 'grid' ? "grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6" : "space-y-2"}>
            {viewMode === 'grid' ? Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[200px] w-full" />)
             : Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[60px] w-full" />)}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Error State */}
        {banksError && (
          <div className="text-destructive flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border border-destructive/50 bg-destructive/10 rounded-md">
            <AlertCircle className="h-5 w-5" />
            <span>Error loading word banks: {banksError.message}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Content Area */}
        {!isLoadingBanks && !banksError && (
          <>
            {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && !filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks created yet. Create one below!</p>
            )}
             {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks match your filter.</p>
            )}

            {/* Grid View */}
            {viewMode === 'grid' && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankCard
                    key={bank.id}
                    id={bank.id}
                    title={bank.name}
                    description={bank.description}
                    wordCount={bank.wordCount}
                    masteryPercentage={bank.masteryPercentage}
                    lastPracticedAt={bank.lastPracticedAt}
                    isShared={bank.isShared}
                    isAdmin={adminGroupIds.includes(bank.groupId ?? '')} // Pass isAdmin based on group membership
                  />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Card (Grid) - Only show if authenticated */}
                {session && (
                  <Card className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full border-dashed min-h-[200px]">
                    <CardContent className="pt-6 text-center">
                      <Plus className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground" />
                      <p className="text-muted-foreground">Create a new word bank</p>
                      <CreateWordBankButton /> {/* Use the new component */}
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* List View */}
            {viewMode === 'list' && (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankListItemComponent key={bank.id} {...bank} />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Button (List) - Only show if authenticated */}

      </div>

      {/* Groups Section */}

'use client'

import { AddWordsModal } from "@/components/features/vocab/AddWordsModal";
import { GroupList } from "@/components/features/vocab/group/GroupList";
import { PracticeHistoryTabContent } from "@/components/features/vocab/PracticeHistoryTabContent";
import { WordBankListItem as WordBankListItemComponent } from "@/components/features/vocab/WordBankListItem";
import { ShareWordBankDialog } from "@/components/features/vocab/ShareWordBankDialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import useLocalStorage from "@/hooks/useLocalStorage";
import { api, type RouterOutputs } from "@/utils/trpc";
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { AlertCircle, BookText, Grid, History, List, Pencil, Play, Plus, Settings, SortAsc, SortDesc, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "next-auth/react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

// Extend word bank type to include groupId for admin check
type WordBankListItem = RouterOutputs['vocab']['listWordBanks'][number] & {
  lastPracticedAt?: Date | null;
  isShared?: boolean;
  groupId?: string | null; // Add groupId as optional
};

type SortKey = 'name' | 'createdAt' | 'masteryPercentage';
type SortDirection = 'asc' | 'desc';

type SessionStatus = 'loading' | 'authenticated' | 'unauthenticated';

function CreateWordBankButton({ isFullWidth = false }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session, status } = useSession();
  const sessionStatus = status as SessionStatus;

  const handleCreateClick = () => {
    if (sessionStatus === 'loading') {
      toast.info('Loading session information...');
      return;
    }
    if (!session) {
      toast.warning('You need to be logged in to create a word bank.');
      router.push('/login?callbackUrl=/vocabulary/create-bank');
      return;
    }
    router.push('/vocabulary/create-bank');
  };

  if (!session) {
    return null;
  }
  
  return (
    <Button
      variant="outline"
      className={isFullWidth ? "w-full mt-4 md:mt-0" : "mt-4"}
      onClick={handleCreateClick}
      disabled={sessionStatus === 'loading'}
    >
      {sessionStatus === 'loading' ? (
        'Loading...'
      ) : (
        isFullWidth ? (
          <><Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Create New Bank</>
        ) : (
          'Create Word Bank'
        )
      )}
    </Button>
  );
}

interface WordBankCardProps {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string | null;
  wordCount: number;
  lastPracticedAt?: Date | null;
  masteryPercentage: number;
  isShared?: boolean;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
}

function WordBankCard({
  id,
  title,
  description,
  wordCount,
  lastPracticedAt,
  masteryPercentage,
  isShared = false,
  isAdmin = false,
}: WordBankCardProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const startSessionMutation = api.vocab.startVocabSession.useMutation({
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      toast.success('Practice session started!');
      router.push(`/vocabulary/practice/${data.practiceSessionId}`);
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      toast.error(`Failed to start session: ${error.message}`);
    }
  });

  const handleStartPractice = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    startSessionMutation.mutate({ wordBankId: id });
  };

  return (
    <Card className="flex flex-col h-full">
     <Link href={`/vocabulary/banks/${id}`} className="flex flex-col flex-grow hover:bg-muted/50 rounded-t-lg">
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <BookText className="h-5 w-5" />
            {title}
            {isShared && <Users className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />}
          </span>
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription className="line-clamp-3 h-[3.75rem] mt-1">
          {description || `Last practiced: ${lastPracticedAt ? format(new Date(lastPracticedAt), 'PP') : 'Never'}`}
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="flex-grow pt-2">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-sm font-medium">
            <span>Mastery</span>
            <span>{masteryPercentage}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-2 bg-secondary rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div className="h-full bg-primary rounded-full" style={{ width: `${masteryPercentage}%` }} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
     </Link>
      <CardFooter className="flex justify-between items-center pt-4">
        {wordCount === 0 ? (
          <AddWordsModal bankId={id}>
            <Button variant="outline" className="w-full border-cyan-500 text-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-500/10 hover:text-cyan-600 flex-grow mr-2">
              <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Add Words
            </Button>
          </AddWordsModal>
        ) : (
          <Button
            variant="default"
            className="w-full flex-grow mr-2 bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-600 text-white"
            onClick={handleStartPractice}
            disabled={startSessionMutation.isPending}
          >
            {startSessionMutation.isPending
              ? 'Starting...'
              : <><Play className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Practice</>
            }
          </Button>
        )}
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/banks/${id}`} passHref>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" title="Manage Words">
             <Settings className="h-4 w-4" />
             <span className="sr-only">Manage Words</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/edit/${id}`} className="ml-1">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" title="Edit Bank">
            <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span className="sr-only">Edit Bank</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        {/* Conditionally render ShareWordBankDialog based on isAdmin */}
        {isAdmin && (
          <ShareWordBankDialog groupId={undefined} wordBanks={[{ id, name: title, description, wordCount }]} />
        )}
      </CardFooter>
    </Card>
  )
}

export default function VocabularyPage() {
  // Use useLocalStorage for viewMode persistence
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useLocalStorage<'grid' | 'list'>('vocabViewMode', 'grid');
  const [filterText, setFilterText] = useState('');
  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState<SortKey>('createdAt');
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<SortDirection>('desc');

  // Fetch word banks
  const { data: wordBanks, isLoading: isLoadingBanks, error: banksError } = api.vocab.listWordBanks.useQuery(undefined, {
    select: (data) => data.map(bank => ({
      ...bank,
      isShared: 'isShared' in bank ? Boolean(bank.isShared) : false
    }))
  })

  // Fetch user's groups for admin status
  const { data: userGroups } = api.group.getUsersGroups.useQuery();

  // Determine admin group IDs for the user
  const adminGroupIds = userGroups?.filter(g => g.members?.some(m => m.userId === g.creatorId && m.role === 'admin')).map(g => g.id) ?? [];

  // Client-side filtering and sorting
  const filteredAndSortedBanks = useMemo(() => {
    if (!wordBanks) return [];
    let banks = [...wordBanks];

    // Filter
    if (filterText) {
      banks = banks.filter(bank =>
        bank.name.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase()) ||
        bank.description?.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    // Sort
    banks.sort((a, b) => {
      let compareA: string | number | Date = a[sortKey];
      let compareB: string | number | Date = b[sortKey];

      if (sortKey === 'createdAt') {
        compareA = new Date(compareA);
        compareB = new Date(compareB);
      }

      let comparison = 0;
      if (compareA > compareB) comparison = 1;
      else if (compareA < compareB) comparison = -1;

      return sortDirection === 'desc' ? comparison * -1 : comparison;
    });

    return banks;
  }, [wordBanks, filterText, sortKey, sortDirection]);

  const toggleSortDirection = () => {
    setSortDirection(prev => prev === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc');
  }

  // Add this near the top of the component
  const { data: session, status: authStatus } = useSession();

  return (
    <main className="container mx-auto px-4 py-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Vocabulary Dashboard</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1">Manage your word banks and track your progress.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Overview Section - Placeholder */}
            {/*
            <Card className="mb-8">
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>Overall Progress</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground">Progress metrics coming soon...</p>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
            */}

      {/* Word Banks Section */}
      <div className="mt-8 mb-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Your Word Banks</h2>
          {/* Filter, Sort, View Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full md:w-auto">
             <Input
               placeholder="Filter banks..."
               value={filterText}
               onChange={(e) => setFilterText(e.target.value)}
               className="h-9 max-w-xs"
             />
             <Select value={sortKey} onValueChange={(value) => setSortKey(value as SortKey)}>
               <SelectTrigger className="h-9 w-[150px]">
                 <SelectValue placeholder="Sort by" />
               </SelectTrigger>
               <SelectContent>
                 <SelectItem value="name">Name</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="createdAt">Date Created</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="masteryPercentage">Mastery</SelectItem>
               </SelectContent>
             </Select>
             <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={toggleSortDirection} className="h-9 w-9">
               {sortDirection === 'asc' ? <SortAsc className="h-4 w-4" /> : <SortDesc className="h-4 w-4" />}
             </Button>
             <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-muted p-1 ml-2">
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'grid' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('grid')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="Grid view">
                 <Grid className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'list' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('list')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="List view">
                 <List className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
             </div>
           </div>
        </div>

        {/* Loading State */}
        {isLoadingBanks && (
          <div className={viewMode === 'grid' ? "grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6" : "space-y-2"}>
            {viewMode === 'grid' ? Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[200px] w-full" />)
             : Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[60px] w-full" />)}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Error State */}
        {banksError && (
          <div className="text-destructive flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border border-destructive/50 bg-destructive/10 rounded-md">
            <AlertCircle className="h-5 w-5" />
            <span>Error loading word banks: {banksError.message}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Content Area */}
        {!isLoadingBanks && !banksError && (
          <>
            {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && !filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks created yet. Create one below!</p>
            )}
             {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks match your filter.</p>
            )}

            {/* Grid View */}
            {viewMode === 'grid' && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankCard
                    key={bank.id}
                    id={bank.id}
                    title={bank.name}
                    description={bank.description}
                    wordCount={bank.wordCount}
                    masteryPercentage={bank.masteryPercentage}
                    lastPracticedAt={bank.lastPracticedAt}
                    isShared={bank.isShared}
                    isAdmin={adminGroupIds.includes(bank.groupId ?? '')} // Pass isAdmin based on group membership
                  />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Card (Grid) - Only show if authenticated */}
                {session && (
                  <Card className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full border-dashed min-h-[200px]">
                    <CardContent className="pt-6 text-center">
                      <Plus className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground" />
                      <p className="text-muted-foreground">Create a new word bank</p>
                      <CreateWordBankButton /> {/* Use the new component */}
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* List View */}
            {viewMode === 'list' && (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankListItemComponent key={bank.id} {...bank} />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Button (List) - Only show if authenticated */}
                {session && (
                  <CreateWordBankButton isFullWidth />
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Groups Section */}
      <div className="mt-12">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Groups</h2>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button variant="outline" asChild>
              <Link href="/vocabulary/groups">
                View All
              </Link>
            </Button>
            <Button asChild>
              <Link href="/vocabulary/groups/create">
                <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                Create Group
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <GroupList />
      </div>

      {/* Practice History Section */}
      <div className="mt-12">
         <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
           <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
             <History className="h-6 w-6" /> Practice History
           </h2>
         </div>
         <PracticeHistoryTabContent />
       </div>
    </main>
  )
}
</apply_diff>

  lastPracticedAt?: Date | null;

  groupId?: string | null; // Add groupId as optional

type SortKey = 'name' | 'createdAt' | 'masteryPercentage';

type SessionStatus = 'loading' | 'authenticated' | 'unauthenticated';

function CreateWordBankButton({ isFullWidth = false }) {

  const handleCreateClick = () => {
    if (sessionStatus === 'loading') {
      toast.info('Loading session information...');
      return;
    }
    if (!session) {
      toast.warning('You need to be logged in to create a word bank.');

  if (!session) {

  return (

  id: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string | null;

  masteryPercentage: number;
  isShared?: boolean;

}

function WordBankCard({
  id,
  title,
  description,
  wordCount,
  lastPracticedAt,
  masteryPercentage,
  isShared = false,
  isAdmin = false,
}: WordBankCardProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const startSessionMutation = api.vocab.startVocabSession.useMutation({
    onSuccess: (data) => {

            variant="default"
            className="w-full flex-grow mr-2 bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-600 text-white"
            onClick={handleStartPractice}
            disabled={startSessionMutation.isPending}
          >
            {startSessionMutation.isPending
              ? 'Starting...'
              : <><Play className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Practice</>
            }

        )}
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/banks/${id}`} passHref>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" title="Manage Words">
             <Settings className="h-4 w-4" />
             <span className="sr-only">Manage Words</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/edit/${id}`} className="ml-1">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" title="Edit Bank">
            <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span className="sr-only">Edit Bank</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        {/* Conditionally render ShareWordBankDialog based on isAdmin */}
        {isAdmin && (
          <ShareWordBankDialog groupId={undefined} wordBanks={[{ id, name: title, description, wordCount }]} />
        )}
      </CardFooter>
    </Card>
  )
}

export default function VocabularyPage() {
  // Use useLocalStorage for viewMode persistence
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useLocalStorage<'grid' | 'list'>('vocabViewMode', 'grid');
  const [filterText, setFilterText] = useState('');
  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState<SortKey>('createdAt');
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<SortDirection>('desc');

  // Fetch word banks
  const { data: wordBanks, isLoading: isLoadingBanks, error: banksError } = api.vocab.listWordBanks.useQuery(undefined, {
    select: (data) => data.map(bank => ({
      ...bank,
      isShared: 'isShared' in bank ? Boolean(bank.isShared) : false
    }))
  })

  // Fetch user's groups for admin status
  const { data: userGroups } = api.group.getUsersGroups.useQuery();

  // Determine admin group IDs for the user
  const adminGroupIds = userGroups?.filter(g => g.members?.some(m => m.userId === g.creatorId && m.role === 'admin')).map(g => g.id) ?? [];

  // Client-side filtering and sorting
  const filteredAndSortedBanks = useMemo(() => {
    if (!wordBanks) return [];
    let banks = [...wordBanks];

    // Filter
    if (filterText) {
      banks = banks.filter(bank =>
        bank.name.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase()) ||
        bank.description?.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    // Sort
    banks.sort((a, b) => {
      let compareA: string | number | Date = a[sortKey];
      let compareB: string | number | Date = b[sortKey];

      if (sortKey === 'createdAt') {
        compareA = new Date(compareA);
        compareB = new Date(compareB);
      }

      let comparison = 0;
      if (compareA > compareB) comparison = 1;
      else if (compareA < compareB) comparison = -1;

      return sortDirection === 'desc' ? comparison * -1 : comparison;
    });

    return banks;
  }, [wordBanks, filterText, sortKey, sortDirection]);

  const toggleSortDirection = () => {
    setSortDirection(prev => prev === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc');
  }

  // Add this near the top of the component
  const { data: session, status: authStatus } = useSession();

  return (
    <main className="container mx-auto px-4 py-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Vocabulary Dashboard</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1">Manage your word banks and track your progress.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Overview Section - Placeholder */}
            {/*
            <Card className="mb-8">
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>Overall Progress</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground">Progress metrics coming soon...</p>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
            */}

      {/* Word Banks Section */}
      <div className="mt-8 mb-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Your Word Banks</h2>
          {/* Filter, Sort, View Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full md:w-auto">
             <Input
               placeholder="Filter banks..."
               value={filterText}
               onChange={(e) => setFilterText(e.target.value)}
               className="h-9 max-w-xs"
             />
             <Select value={sortKey} onValueChange={(value) => setSortKey(value as SortKey)}>
               <SelectTrigger className="h-9 w-[150px]">
                 <SelectValue placeholder="Sort by" />
               </SelectTrigger>
               <SelectContent>
                 <SelectItem value="name">Name</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="createdAt">Date Created</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="masteryPercentage">Mastery</SelectItem>
               </SelectContent>
             </Select>
             <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={toggleSortDirection} className="h-9 w-9">
               {sortDirection === 'asc' ? <SortAsc className="h-4 w-4" /> : <SortDesc className="h-4 w-4" />}
             </Button>
             <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-muted p-1 ml-2">
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'grid' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('grid')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="Grid view">
                 <Grid className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'list' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('list')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="List view">
                 <List className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
             </div>
           </div>
        </div>

        {/* Loading State */}
        {isLoadingBanks && (
          <div className={viewMode === 'grid' ? "grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6" : "space-y-2"}>
            {viewMode === 'grid' ? Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[200px] w-full" />)
             : Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[60px] w-full" />)}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Error State */}
        {banksError && (
          <div className="text-destructive flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border border-destructive/50 bg-destructive/10 rounded-md">
            <AlertCircle className="h-5 w-5" />
            <span>Error loading word banks: {banksError.message}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Content Area */}
        {!isLoadingBanks && !banksError && (
          <>
            {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && !filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks created yet. Create one below!</p>
            )}
             {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks match your filter.</p>
            )}

            {/* Grid View */}
            {viewMode === 'grid' && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankCard
                    key={bank.id}
                    id={bank.id}
                    title={bank.name}
                    description={bank.description}
                    wordCount={bank.wordCount}
                    masteryPercentage={bank.masteryPercentage}
                    lastPracticedAt={bank.lastPracticedAt}
                    isShared={bank.isShared}
                    isAdmin={adminGroupIds.includes(bank.groupId ?? '')} // Pass isAdmin based on group membership
                  />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Card (Grid) - Only show if authenticated */}
                {session && (
                  <Card className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full border-dashed min-h-[200px]">
                    <CardContent className="pt-6 text-center">
                      <Plus className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground" />
                      <p className="text-muted-foreground">Create a new word bank</p>
                      <CreateWordBankButton /> {/* Use the new component */}
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* List View */}
            {viewMode === 'list' && (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankListItemComponent key={bank.id} {...bank} />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Button (List) - Only show if authenticated */}
                {session && (
                  <CreateWordBankButton isFullWidth />
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Groups Section */}
      <div className="mt-12">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Groups</h2>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button variant="outline" asChild>
              <Link href="/vocabulary/groups">
                View All
              </Link>
            </Button>
            <Button asChild>
              <Link href="/vocabulary/groups/create">
                <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                Create Group
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <GroupList />
      </div>

      {/* Practice History Section */}
      <div className="mt-12">
         <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
           <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
             <History className="h-6 w-6" /> Practice History
           </h2>
         </div>
         <PracticeHistoryTabContent />
       </div>
    </main>
  )
}
</content>

'use client'

import { AddWordsModal } from "@/components/features/vocab/AddWordsModal";
import { GroupList } from "@/components/features/vocab/group/GroupList";
import { PracticeHistoryTabContent } from "@/components/features/vocab/PracticeHistoryTabContent";
import { WordBankListItem as WordBankListItemComponent } from "@/components/features/vocab/WordBankListItem";
import { ShareWordBankDialog } from "@/components/features/vocab/ShareWordBankDialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import useLocalStorage from "@/hooks/useLocalStorage";
import { api, type RouterOutputs } from "@/utils/trpc";
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { AlertCircle, BookText, Grid, History, List, Pencil, Play, Plus, Settings, SortAsc, SortDesc, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "next-auth/react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

// Extend word bank type to include groupId for admin check
type WordBankListItem = RouterOutputs['vocab']['listWordBanks'][number] & {
  lastPracticedAt?: Date | null;
  isShared?: boolean;
  groupId?: string | null; // Add groupId as optional
};

type SortKey = 'name' | 'createdAt' | 'masteryPercentage';
type SortDirection = 'asc' | 'desc';

type SessionStatus = 'loading' | 'authenticated' | 'unauthenticated';

function CreateWordBankButton({ isFullWidth = false }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session, status } = useSession();
  const sessionStatus = status as SessionStatus;

  const handleCreateClick = () => {
    if (sessionStatus === 'loading') {
      toast.info('Loading session information...');
      return;
    }
    if (!session) {
      toast.warning('You need to be logged in to create a word bank.');
      router.push('/login?callbackUrl=/vocabulary/create-bank');
      return;
    }
    router.push('/vocabulary/create-bank');
  };

  if (!session) {
    return null;
  }
  
  return (
    <Button
      variant="outline"
      className={isFullWidth ? "w-full mt-4 md:mt-0" : "mt-4"}
      onClick={handleCreateClick}
      disabled={sessionStatus === 'loading'}
    >
      {sessionStatus === 'loading' ? (
        'Loading...'
      ) : (
        isFullWidth ? (
          <><Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Create New Bank</>
        ) : (
          'Create Word Bank'
        )
      )}
    </Button>
  );
}

interface WordBankCardProps {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string | null;
  wordCount: number;
  lastPracticedAt?: Date | null;
  masteryPercentage: number;
  isShared?: boolean;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
  groupId?: string | null;
}

function WordBankCard({
  id,
  title,
  description,
  wordCount,
  lastPracticedAt,
  masteryPercentage,
  isShared = false,
  isAdmin = false,
  groupId = null,
}: WordBankCardProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const startSessionMutation = api.vocab.startVocabSession.useMutation({
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      toast.success('Practice session started!');
      router.push(`/vocabulary/practice/${data.practiceSessionId}`);
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      toast.error(`Failed to start session: ${error.message}`);
    }
  });

  const handleStartPractice = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    startSessionMutation.mutate({ wordBankId: id });
  };

  return (
    <Card className="flex flex-col h-full">
     <Link href={`/vocabulary/banks/${id}`} className="flex flex-col flex-grow hover:bg-muted/50 rounded-t-lg">
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <BookText className="h-5 w-5" />
            {title}
            {isShared && <Users className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />}
          </span>
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription className="line-clamp-3 h-[3.75rem] mt-1">
          {description || `Last practiced: ${lastPracticedAt ? format(new Date(lastPracticedAt), 'PP') : 'Never'}`}
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="flex-grow pt-2">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-sm font-medium">
            <span>Mastery</span>
            <span>{masteryPercentage}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-2 bg-secondary rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div className="h-full bg-primary rounded-full" style={{ width: `${masteryPercentage}%` }} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
     </Link>
      <CardFooter className="flex justify-between items-center pt-4">
        {wordCount === 0 ? (
          <AddWordsModal bankId={id}>
            <Button variant="outline" className="w-full border-cyan-500 text-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-500/10 hover:text-cyan-600 flex-grow mr-2">
              <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Add Words
            </Button>
          </AddWordsModal>
        ) : (
          <Button
            variant="default"
            className="w-full flex-grow mr-2 bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-600 text-white"
            onClick={handleStartPractice}
            disabled={startSessionMutation.isPending}
          >
            {startSessionMutation.isPending
              ? 'Starting...'
              : <><Play className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Practice</>
            }
          </Button>
        )}
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/banks/${id}`} passHref>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" title="Manage Words">
             <Settings className="h-4 w-4" />
             <span className="sr-only">Manage Words</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/edit/${id}`} className="ml-1">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" title="Edit Bank">
            <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span className="sr-only">Edit Bank</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        {/* Conditionally render ShareWordBankDialog based on isAdmin */}
        {isAdmin && (
          <ShareWordBankDialog groupId={groupId} wordBanks={[{ id, name: title, description, wordCount }]} />
        )}
      </CardFooter>
    </Card>
  )
}

export default function VocabularyPage() {
  // Use useLocalStorage for viewMode persistence
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useLocalStorage<'grid' | 'list'>('vocabViewMode', 'grid');
  const [filterText, setFilterText] = useState('');
  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState<SortKey>('createdAt');
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<SortDirection>('desc');

  // Fetch word banks
  const { data: wordBanks, isLoading: isLoadingBanks, error: banksError } = api.vocab.listWordBanks.useQuery(undefined, {
    select: (data) => data.map(bank => ({
      ...bank,
      isShared: 'isShared' in bank ? Boolean(bank.isShared) : false,
      groupId: (bank as any).groupId ?? null // Ensure groupId is included, cast to any to avoid type error
    }))
  })

  // Fetch user's groups for admin status
  const { data: userGroups } = api.group.getUsersGroups.useQuery();

  // Determine admin group IDs for the user
  const adminGroupIds = userGroups?.filter(g => g.members?.some(m => m.userId === g.creatorId && m.role === 'admin')).map(g => g.id) ?? [];

  // Client-side filtering and sorting
  const filteredAndSortedBanks = useMemo(() => {
    if (!wordBanks) return [];
    let banks = [...wordBanks];

    // Filter
    if (filterText) {
      banks = banks.filter(bank =>
        bank.name.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase()) ||
        bank.description?.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    // Sort
    banks.sort((a, b) => {
      let compareA: string | number | Date = a[sortKey];
      let compareB: string | number | Date = b[sortKey];

      if (sortKey === 'createdAt') {
        compareA = new Date(compareA);
        compareB = new Date(compareB);
      }

      let comparison = 0;
      if (compareA > compareB) comparison = 1;
      else if (compareA < compareB) comparison = -1;

      return sortDirection === 'desc' ? comparison * -1 : comparison;
    });

    return banks;
  }, [wordBanks, filterText, sortKey, sortDirection]);

  const toggleSortDirection = () => {
    setSortDirection(prev => prev === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc');
  }

  // Add this near the top of the component
  const { data: session, status: authStatus } = useSession();

  return (
    <main className="container mx-auto px-4 py-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Vocabulary Dashboard</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1">Manage your word banks and track your progress.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Overview Section - Placeholder */}
            {/*
            <Card className="mb-8">
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>Overall Progress</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground">Progress metrics coming soon...</p>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
            */}

      {/* Word Banks Section */}
      <div className="mt-8 mb-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Your Word Banks</h2>
          {/* Filter, Sort, View Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full md:w-auto">
             <Input
               placeholder="Filter banks..."
               value={filterText}
               onChange={(e) => setFilterText(e.target.value)}
               className="h-9 max-w-xs"
             />
             <Select value={sortKey} onValueChange={(value) => setSortKey(value as SortKey)}>
               <SelectTrigger className="h-9 w-[150px]">
                 <SelectValue placeholder="Sort by" />
               </SelectTrigger>
               <SelectContent>
                 <SelectItem value="name">Name</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="createdAt">Date Created</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="masteryPercentage">Mastery</SelectItem>
               </SelectContent>
             </Select>
             <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={toggleSortDirection} className="h-9 w-9">
               {sortDirection === 'asc' ? <SortAsc className="h-4 w-4" /> : <SortDesc className="h-4 w-4" />}
             </Button>
             <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-muted p-1 ml-2">
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'grid' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('grid')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="Grid view">
                 <Grid className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'list' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('list')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="List view">
                 <List className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
             </div>
           </div>
        </div>

        {/* Loading State */}
        {isLoadingBanks && (
          <div className={viewMode === 'grid' ? "grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6" : "space-y-2"}>
            {viewMode === 'grid' ? Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[200px] w-full" />)
             : Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[60px] w-full" />)}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Error State */}
        {banksError && (
          <div className="text-destructive flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border border-destructive/50 bg-destructive/10 rounded-md">
            <AlertCircle className="h-5 w-5" />
            <span>Error loading word banks: {banksError.message}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Content Area */}
        {!isLoadingBanks && !banksError && (
          <>
            {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && !filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks created yet. Create one below!</p>
            )}
             {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks match your filter.</p>
            )}

            {/* Grid View */}
            {viewMode === 'grid' && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankCard
                    key={bank.id}
                    id={bank.id}
                    title={bank.name}
                    description={bank.description}
                    wordCount={bank.wordCount}
                    masteryPercentage={bank.masteryPercentage}
                    lastPracticedAt={bank.lastPracticedAt}
                    isShared={bank.isShared}
                    isAdmin={adminGroupIds.includes(bank.groupId ?? '')}
                    groupId={bank.groupId ?? null}
                  />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Card (Grid) - Only show if authenticated */}
                {session && (
                  <Card className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full border-dashed min-h-[200px]">
                    <CardContent className="pt-6 text-center">
                      <Plus className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground" />
                      <p className="text-muted-foreground">Create a new word bank</p>
                      <CreateWordBankButton />
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* List View */}
            {viewMode === 'list' && (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankListItemComponent
                    key={bank.id}
                    {...bank}
                    isAdmin={adminGroupIds.includes(bank.groupId ?? '')}
                    groupId={bank.groupId ?? null}
                  />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Button (List) - Only show if authenticated */}
                {session && <CreateWordBankButton isFullWidth />}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Groups Section */}
      <div className="mt-12">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Groups</h2>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button variant="outline" asChild>
              <Link href="/vocabulary/groups">View All</Link>
            </Button>
            <Button asChild>
              <Link href="/vocabulary/groups/create">
                <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                Create Group
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <GroupList />
      </div>

      {/* Practice History Section */}
      <div className="mt-12">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
            <History className="h-6 w-6" /> Practice History
          </h2>
        </div>
        <PracticeHistoryTabContent />
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

import { AddWordsModal } from "@/components/features/vocab/AddWordsModal";
import { GroupList } from "@/components/features/vocab/group/GroupList";

import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

// Extend word bank type to include groupId for admin check
type WordBankListItem = RouterOutputs['vocab']['listWordBanks'][number] & {
  lastPracticedAt?: Date | null;
  isShared?: boolean;
  groupId?: string | null; // Add groupId as optional
};

type SortKey = 'name' | 'createdAt' | 'masteryPercentage';
type SortDirection = 'asc' | 'desc';

type SessionStatus = 'loading' | 'authenticated' | 'unauthenticated';

function CreateWordBankButton({ isFullWidth = false }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session, status } = useSession();
  const sessionStatus = status as SessionStatus;

  const handleCreateClick = () => {
    if (sessionStatus === 'loading') {
      toast.info('Loading session information...');
      return;
    }
    if (!session) {
      toast.warning('You need to be logged in to create a word bank.');
      router.push('/login?callbackUrl=/vocabulary/create-bank');
      return;
    }
    router.push('/vocabulary/create-bank');
  };

  if (!session) {
    return null;
  }
  
  return (
    <Button
      variant="outline"
      className={isFullWidth ? "w-full mt-4 md:mt-0" : "mt-4"}
      onClick={handleCreateClick}
      disabled={sessionStatus === 'loading'}
    >
      {sessionStatus === 'loading' ? (
        'Loading...'
      ) : (
        isFullWidth ? (
          <><Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Create New Bank</>
        ) : (
          'Create Word Bank'
        )
      )}
    </Button>
  );
}

interface WordBankCardProps {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string | null;
  wordCount: number;
  lastPracticedAt?: Date | null;
  masteryPercentage: number;
  isShared?: boolean;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
  groupId?: string | null;
}

function WordBankCard({
  id,
  title,
  description,
  wordCount,
  lastPracticedAt,
  masteryPercentage,
  isShared = false,
  isAdmin = false,
  groupId = null,
}: WordBankCardProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const startSessionMutation = api.vocab.startVocabSession.useMutation({
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      toast.success('Practice session started!');
      router.push(`/vocabulary/practice/${data.practiceSessionId}`);
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      toast.error(`Failed to start session: ${error.message}`);
    }
  });

  const handleStartPractice = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    startSessionMutation.mutate({ wordBankId: id });
  };

  return (
    <Card className="flex flex-col h-full">
     <Link href={`/vocabulary/banks/${id}`} className="flex flex-col flex-grow hover:bg-muted/50 rounded-t-lg">
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <BookText className="h-5 w-5" />
            {title}
            {isShared && <Users className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />}
          </span>
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription className="line-clamp-3 h-[3.75rem] mt-1">
          {description || `Last practiced: ${lastPracticedAt ? format(new Date(lastPracticedAt), 'PP') : 'Never'}`}
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="flex-grow pt-2">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-sm font-medium">
            <span>Mastery</span>
            <span>{masteryPercentage}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-2 bg-secondary rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div className="h-full bg-primary rounded-full" style={{ width: `${masteryPercentage}%` }} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
     </Link>
      <CardFooter className="flex justify-between items-center pt-4">
        {wordCount === 0 ? (
          <AddWordsModal bankId={id}>
            <Button variant="outline" className="w-full border-cyan-500 text-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-500/10 hover:text-cyan-600 flex-grow mr-2">
              <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Add Words
            </Button>
          </AddWordsModal>
        ) : (
          <Button
            variant="default"
            className="w-full flex-grow mr-2 bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-600 text-white"
            onClick={handleStartPractice}
            disabled={startSessionMutation.isPending}
          >
            {startSessionMutation.isPending
              ? 'Starting...'
              : <><Play className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Practice</>
            }
          </Button>
        )}
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/banks/${id}`} passHref>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" title="Manage Words">
             <Settings className="h-4 w-4" />
             <span className="sr-only">Manage Words</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        <Link href={`/vocabulary/edit/${id}`} className="ml-1">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" title="Edit Bank">
            <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span className="sr-only">Edit Bank</span>
          </Button>
        </Link>
        {/* Conditionally render ShareWordBankDialog based on isAdmin */}
        {isAdmin && (
          <ShareWordBankDialog groupId={groupId} wordBanks={[{ id, name: title, description, wordCount }]} />
        )}
      </CardFooter>
    </Card>
  )
}

export default function VocabularyPage() {
  // Use useLocalStorage for viewMode persistence
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useLocalStorage<'grid' | 'list'>('vocabViewMode', 'grid');
  const [filterText, setFilterText] = useState('');
  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState<SortKey>('createdAt');
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<SortDirection>('desc');

  // Fetch word banks
  const { data: wordBanks, isLoading: isLoadingBanks, error: banksError } = api.vocab.listWordBanks.useQuery(undefined, {
    select: (data) => data.map(bank => ({
      ...bank,
      isShared: 'isShared' in bank ? Boolean(bank.isShared) : false,
      groupId: (bank as any).groupId ?? null // Ensure groupId is included, cast to any to avoid type error
    }))
  })

  // Fetch user's groups for admin status
  const { data: userGroups } = api.group.getUsersGroups.useQuery();

  // Determine admin group IDs for the user
  const adminGroupIds = userGroups?.filter(g => g.members?.some(m => m.userId === g.creatorId && m.role === 'admin')).map(g => g.id) ?? [];

  // Client-side filtering and sorting
  const filteredAndSortedBanks = useMemo(() => {
    if (!wordBanks) return [];
    let banks = [...wordBanks];

    // Filter
    if (filterText) {
      banks = banks.filter(bank =>
        bank.name.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase()) ||
        bank.description?.toLowerCase().includes(filterText.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    // Sort
    banks.sort((a, b) => {
      let compareA: string | number | Date = a[sortKey];
      let compareB: string | number | Date = b[sortKey];

      if (sortKey === 'createdAt') {
        compareA = new Date(compareA);
        compareB = new Date(compareB);
      }

      let comparison = 0;
      if (compareA > compareB) comparison = 1;
      else if (compareA < compareB) comparison = -1;

      return sortDirection === 'desc' ? comparison * -1 : comparison;
    });

    return banks;
  }, [wordBanks, filterText, sortKey, sortDirection]);

  const toggleSortDirection = () => {
    setSortDirection(prev => prev === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc');
  }

  // Add this near the top of the component
  const { data: session, status: authStatus } = useSession();

  return (
    <main className="container mx-auto px-4 py-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Vocabulary Dashboard</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1">Manage your word banks and track your progress.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Overview Section - Placeholder */}
            {/*
            <Card className="mb-8">
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>Overall Progress</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <p className="text-muted-foreground">Progress metrics coming soon...</p>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
            */}

      {/* Word Banks Section */}
      <div className="mt-8 mb-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Your Word Banks</h2>
          {/* Filter, Sort, View Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full md:w-auto">
             <Input
               placeholder="Filter banks..."
               value={filterText}
               onChange={(e) => setFilterText(e.target.value)}
               className="h-9 max-w-xs"
             />
             <Select value={sortKey} onValueChange={(value) => setSortKey(value as SortKey)}>
               <SelectTrigger className="h-9 w-[150px]">
                 <SelectValue placeholder="Sort by" />
               </SelectTrigger>
               <SelectContent>
                 <SelectItem value="name">Name</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="createdAt">Date Created</SelectItem>
                 <SelectItem value="masteryPercentage">Mastery</SelectItem>
               </SelectContent>
             </Select>
             <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={toggleSortDirection} className="h-9 w-9">
               {sortDirection === 'asc' ? <SortAsc className="h-4 w-4" /> : <SortDesc className="h-4 w-4" />}
             </Button>
             <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-muted p-1 ml-2">
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'grid' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('grid')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="Grid view">
                 <Grid className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
               <Button
                 variant={viewMode === 'list' ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
                 size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode('list')} className="h-7 px-2" aria-label="List view">
                 <List className="h-4 w-4" />
               </Button>
             </div>
           </div>
        </div>

        {/* Loading State */}
        {isLoadingBanks && (
          <div className={viewMode === 'grid' ? "grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6" : "space-y-2"}>
            {viewMode === 'grid' ? Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[200px] w-full" />)
             : Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-[60px] w-full" />)}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Error State */}
        {banksError && (
          <div className="text-destructive flex items-center gap-2 p-4 border border-destructive/50 bg-destructive/10 rounded-md">
            <AlertCircle className="h-5 w-5" />
            <span>Error loading word banks: {banksError.message}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Content Area */}
        {!isLoadingBanks && !banksError && (
          <>
            {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && !filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks created yet. Create one below!</p>
            )}
             {filteredAndSortedBanks.length === 0 && filterText && (
               <p className="text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">No word banks match your filter.</p>
            )}

            {/* Grid View */}
            {viewMode === 'grid' && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankCard
                    key={bank.id}
                    id={bank.id}
                    title={bank.name}
                    description={bank.description}
                    wordCount={bank.wordCount}
                    masteryPercentage={bank.masteryPercentage}
                    lastPracticedAt={bank.lastPracticedAt}
                    isShared={bank.isShared}
                    isAdmin={adminGroupIds.includes(bank.groupId ?? '')}
                    groupId={bank.groupId ?? null}
                  />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Card (Grid) - Only show if authenticated */}
                {session && (
                  <Card className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full border-dashed min-h-[200px]">
                    <CardContent className="pt-6 text-center">
                      <Plus className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 text-muted-foreground" />
                      <p className="text-muted-foreground">Create a new word bank</p>
                      <CreateWordBankButton />
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* List View */}
            {viewMode === 'list' && (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {filteredAndSortedBanks.map((bank) => (
                  <WordBankListItemComponent
                    key={bank.id}
                    {...bank}
                    isAdmin={adminGroupIds.includes(bank.groupId ?? '')}
                    groupId={bank.groupId ?? null}
                  />
                ))}
                {/* Create New Bank Button (List) - Only show if authenticated */}
                {session && <CreateWordBankButton isFullWidth />}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Groups Section */}
      <div className="mt-12">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Groups</h2>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button variant="outline" asChild>
              <Link href="/vocabulary/groups">View All</Link>
            </Button>
            <Button asChild>
              <Link href="/vocabulary/groups/create">
                <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                Create Group
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <GroupList />
      </div>

      {/* Practice History Section */}
      <div className="mt-12">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
            <History className="h-6 w-6" /> Practice History
          </h2>
        </div>
        <PracticeHistoryTabContent />
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
</content>

**Assistant:**

The logic for hiding the "Share With" option is implemented in the `WordBankCard` and `WordBankListItem` components by conditionally rendering the `ShareWordBankDialog` only if the `isAdmin` prop is true.

In `src/app/vocabulary/page.tsx`, the `isAdmin` prop is computed by checking if the current user is an admin of the group that owns the word bank. This is done by fetching the user's groups via `api.group.getUsersGroups.useQuery()`, filtering for groups where the user has an admin role, and then checking if the word bank's `groupId` is in the list of admin group IDs.

Specifically:
- `isAdmin` is set to `true` if `adminGroupIds.includes(bank.groupId ?? '')` is true.
- The `ShareWordBankDialog` is only rendered if `isAdmin` is true.

This ensures that only group admins see the "Share With" option and can generate share links.

If you want, I can help you verify or modify this logic further.

<thinking>The `Group` model does not have a direct relation to `WordBank`. Instead, there is a `SharedWordBank` model that links `WordBank` and `Group` via `sharedWithGroupId`.

This means word banks are shared with groups via the `SharedWordBank` relation, not owned by groups directly.

Therefore, to determine if a user is an admin of a group that has access to a word bank, we need to check if the user is an admin of any group that the word bank is shared with.

This requires a more complex query or data structure to map word banks to groups they are shared with and the user's roles in those groups.

In the current frontend code, the `groupId` property on word banks is not reliable for this check.

I recommend updating the backend API to provide word banks along with the groups they are shared with, including the user's role in those groups, so the frontend can accurately determine admin status.

Alternatively, the frontend can fetch the user's groups and shared word banks separately and compute the admin status accordingly.

<result>
The current logic for hiding the "Share With" option in the frontend is based on checking if the user is an admin of the group that owns the word bank via a `groupId` property on the word bank.

However, the Prisma schema shows that word banks are not directly owned by groups. Instead, word banks are shared with groups via the `SharedWordBank` model, which links word banks and groups.

Therefore, the `groupId` property on word banks is not reliable for determining group ownership or admin status.

To accurately determine if a user should see the "Share With" option, the frontend needs to know which groups a word bank is shared with and the user's role in those groups.

I recommend enhancing the backend API to provide word banks along with their shared groups and the user's roles in those groups, or alternatively, fetching the user's groups and shared word banks separately and computing admin status in the frontend.

The current implementation's logic for hiding the "Share With" option is insufficient due to the data model and requires these improvements for correct permission handling.
</result>
